## 🧭 Reflow Risk (WCAG 1.4.10)

Page-level indicator: resizes to 320 CSS pixels wide and checks for horizontal overflow. This is a risk indicator, not a conformance verdict — see [Behavioral Accessibility Automation](https://github.com/mgifford/ACCESSIBILITY.md/blob/main/examples/BEHAVIORAL_ACCESSIBILITY_AUTOMATION.md) for what it can and cannot detect.

### https://www.aartselaar.be/

#### Rule: [aria-hidden-focus](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.11/aria-hidden-focus?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**WCAG**: [SC 4.1.2](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/name-role-value) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-07-26
- Message: ARIA hidden element must not be focusable or contain focusable elements
- HTML: `<a class="contentblocks__hidden-btn" aria-hidden="true" href="/nieuwsbrieven/inschrijven"><span>Abonneer je</span></a>`
